# Plugins · A · part 5

1,319 entries, agentic-architecture-skills to AI Agent Prompts. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **agentic-architecture-skills**: The published architecture skills, one install for the whole catalogue. ([muralisid/agentic-architecture-skills](https://github.com/muralisid/agentic-architecture-skills) · 12 skills)
- **agentic-atlas**: Do design work with Agentic Atlas over hosted MCP at agentic-atlas.dev/mcp/. Three skills — audit an existing agent artifact, design a new agent system, apply one named pattern — over two read-only agents; no SDK, CLI, local server, or content bundle is installed. ([aj604/agentic-atlas-plugin](https://github.com/aj604/agentic-atlas-plugin) · ★5 · 3 skills, 2 agents, 1 MCP config)
- **agentic-atlas**: Profile agentic development approaches on 13 signed, diverging axes; deterministic engine, no API key. ([adamcaviness/agentic-atlas](https://github.com/adamcaviness/agentic-atlas) · 59 skills, 5 agents)
- **agentic-audit-suite**: Stateful codebase audit, remediation queue, docs drift and UX review as nine namespaced skills. ([mihaimyh/agentic-audit-suite](https://github.com/mihaimyh/agentic-audit-suite) · 9 skills)
- **agentic-auto-improve**: The Data-First Engineering Protocol as an installable asset set: 34 skills, 15 agents and 14 hooks that put design before implementation and tests before code. ([lneninger/claude-agentic-auto-improve](https://github.com/lneninger/claude-agentic-auto-improve) · 34 skills, 15 agents, 1 hook file)
- **agentic-awesome-skills**: Expose the plugin-safe Claude Code subset of Agentic Awesome Skills through a single marketplace entry. - **agentic-backlog-kit**: Deterministic GitHub Issues and Projects backlog workflows for agents ([aegolius-labs/agentic-backlog-kit](https://github.com/aegolius-labs/agentic-backlog-kit) · 6 skills)
- **agentic-behavior**: Skills for configuring Claude Code, behavior correction, memory tracking, autonomy rules, time-context awareness, and session exit/restart. Combines prompt history tracking, git-backed memory sync, the self-checking Ralph loop pattern, and graceful session exit via SIGINT. ([nsheaps/ai-mktpl](https://github.com/nsheaps/ai-mktpl/tree/HEAD/plugins/agentic-behavior) · ★3 · 9 skills, 1 hook file)
- **agentic-beta-testers**: AI-powered browser testing against any URL using personality-driven agents -- speedrunners, chaos gremlins, skeptical executives -- each surfacing different categories of usability and reliability issues. ([rjspence3/claude-beta-tester](https://github.com/rjspence3/claude-beta-tester) · ★1 · 2 commands)
- **agentic-bi-ops**: DEPRECATED alias — renamed to agentic-board. Points to the same plugin so existing `agentic-bi-ops` installs keep updating. Install the new name: agentic-board. - **agentic-board**: Run coding agents off your real GitHub Projects board: a quota-aware, review-gated coordinator, not another local Kanban. ([CSalcedoDataBI/agentic-board](https://github.com/CSalcedoDataBI/agentic-board/tree/HEAD/plugins/agentic-board) · ★3 · 15 skills, 8 commands, 1 hook file)
- **agentic-bootstrap**: Bootstrap or adopt the agentic-mode documentation pipeline in any repo — a layered, ID-joined, harness-neutral set of collaboration docs (AGENTS.md, REQUIREMENTS, USER_GUIDE/API_REFERENCE, VALIDATION, WORKFLOW) plus a config-driven checker and CI gate. Self-contained: vendors the RUNBOOK, doctrine, templates, and checker. ([madcatone/agentic-mode](https://github.com/madcatone/agentic-mode/tree/HEAD/adapters/claude-code/plugins/agentic-bootstrap) · ★1 · 1 skill)
